\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{k}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{k}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|=o_{p}(1).

Moreover, we see

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{k}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right||\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\biggl(\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{k}^{*})(A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-A_{i-1}(\alpha_{k}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\biggr)\right||\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{k}^{*})(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*})\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[A_{i-1}^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{k}^{*})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{k}^{*}+u(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*}))\right]\right)_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\Bigr)\Biggr||\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\biggl(\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*})+\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{k}^{*}-\alpha_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*})\otimes(\alpha_{k}^{*}-\alpha_{0})\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left(\mathrm{tr}\left[A_{i-1}^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{k}^{*})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{k}^{*}+u(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*}))\right]\right)_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\biggr)\Biggr||\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{\sqrt{n}}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\right|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+L/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0}|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+L/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)(\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|)^{2}\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+L/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\vartheta_{\alpha}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{h}{\sqrt{n}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}\right)

(7.9)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

Therefore, from (
7.5
)-(
7.9
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}|{\mathcal{D}}_{n}^{\alpha}(v)|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

By the similar proof, we see

\sup_{v\in[-L,0]}|{\mathcal{D}}_{n}^{\alpha}(v)|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0

and this proof is complete.
∎

Suppose that
[C1]
-
[C5]
,
[C6-I]
,

[A1-I]
and
[A2-I]
hold.
Then, for all
L>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{n}(v)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathbb{F}(v)\text{ in }\mathbb{D}[-L,L]

as
n\to\infty
.

Proof.

We consider
v>0
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{n}(v)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(F_{i}(\alpha_{1}^{*})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\biggl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{2}}{2}\partial_{\alpha}^{3}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 3}\biggr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right)+\bar{o}_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=:\mathbb{F}_{1,n}(v)+\mathbb{F}_{2,n}(v)+\bar{o}_{p}(1),

where
Y_{n}(v)=\bar{o}_{p}(1)
denotes
\sup_{v\in[0,L]}|Y_{n}(v)|=o_{p}(1)
.
Now, we see

(7.10)

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{1,n}(v)=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})+\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}](\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*}),

(7.11)

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}](\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})\right|\leq\vartheta_{\alpha}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+L/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(h,\theta)=O_{p}\left(\frac{h}{\vartheta_{\alpha}}\right)=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\biggl(\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})\biggr)^{2}\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\biggl[\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)^{\mathsf{T}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)\biggr|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\biggr](\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle=(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\left(2\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\right)(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

(7.12)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}2e_{\alpha}^{\mathsf{T}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Xi^{\alpha}(x,\alpha_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\alpha_{0}}(x)e_{\alpha}v=4\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v

and

(7.13)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\biggl(\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})\biggr)^{4}\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{4}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

According to Corollary 3.8 of McLeish (1974), we obtain,
from (
7.12
) and (
7.13
),

(7.14)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}^{1/2}\mathcal{W}(v)\text{ in }\mathbb{D}[0,L].

Further, from (
7.10
), (
7.11
) and (
7.14
), we have

\mathbb{F}_{1,n}(v)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}^{1/2}\mathcal{W}(v)
in
\mathbb{D}[0,L]
.

Besides, we see

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\Bigr)\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right|

(7.15)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|,

(7.16)

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\Bigr)\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0

and

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\left(\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\right)\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|+o_{p}(1)

(7.17)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0,

where (
7.16
) is obtained by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\Bigr)\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\vartheta_{\alpha}^{4}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+L/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\left|\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\alpha}^{2}F_{i}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}\longrightarrow 0

from Theorem 2.11 of Hall and Heyde (1980),
and (
7.17
) is obtained by

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in[0,\epsilon_{n}]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{v\in[\epsilon_{n},L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{n}\sup_{v\in[0,\epsilon_{n}]}\left|\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+L\sup_{v\in[\epsilon_{n},L]}\left|\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}+v/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}]}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(\epsilon_{n})+L\max_{[n^{1/r}]\leq k\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+k}\Xi^{\alpha}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}\right|

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

Here
\{\epsilon_{n}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
is a positive sequence such that

\epsilon_{n}\longrightarrow 0
and
\epsilon_{n}h/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}\longrightarrow\infty
,
and

r
is a constant with

r\in(1,2)
and
nh^{r}\longrightarrow\infty
.
From (
7.15
)-(
7.17
), we have

\sup_{v\in[0,L]}|\mathbb{F}_{2,n}(v)-\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
.
Therefore we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{n}(v)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}-2\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}^{1/2}\mathcal{W}(v)+\mathcal{J}_{\alpha}v\ \text{ in }\ \mathbb{D}[0,L].

The argument for
v<0
is proved as well.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1
.

Let
M\geq 1
. We have

(7.18)

\displaystyle P\left(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}|\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha}|>M\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\right)+P\left(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}-\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha})>M\right).

For
\tau>\tau_{*}^{\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})-\Phi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau]}F_{i}(\alpha_{1})+\sum_{i=[n\tau]+1}^{n}F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{n}F_{i}(\alpha_{2})

(7.19)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Bigl(F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})\Bigr).

Now, from

\displaystyle F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})

\displaystyle=F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2})-I_{d}\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2})

\displaystyle\quad-\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2})-h^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\Bigr),

we obtain

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})-\Phi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Bigl(F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2})-I_{d}\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2})-h^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\Bigr)

\displaystyle=:{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})+{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})+{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}).

Let
D_{n,M}^{\alpha}=\{\tau\in[0,1]|n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\tau-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\}
.
For all
\delta>0
,
we have

\displaystyle P\left(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\Phi_{n}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})\leq\Phi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\Bigl(\Phi_{n}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})-\Phi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\Bigl({\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})+{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})+{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})+{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})+{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq-\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq-\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle=:P_{1,n}^{\alpha}+P_{2,n}^{\alpha}+P_{3,n}^{\alpha}.

[i] Evaluation of
P_{1,n}^{\alpha}
.
Choose
\epsilon>0
as you like.
Let

\mathcal{O}_{\alpha}
be an open neighborhood of
\alpha
.
Because
\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)
is continuous with respect to
\alpha\in\Theta_{A}
,
we can choose
\bar{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\hat{\alpha}_{2}}
so that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(F_{i}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-F_{i}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-\left.\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right|_{\alpha_{k}=\hat{\alpha}_{k}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\bar{\alpha})-\left.\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right|_{\alpha=\bar{\alpha}}\right)(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}).

If
\hat{\alpha}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}
, then

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\bar{\alpha})-\left.\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right|_{\alpha=\bar{\alpha}}\right)\right||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\alpha\in\Theta_{A}}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle=:\sup_{\alpha\in\Theta_{A}}|\mathbb{M}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha)||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|.

Hence we have

\displaystyle P_{1,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta,\ |\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\leq 2\vartheta_{\alpha},\ \hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\alpha})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{\sup_{\alpha\in\Theta_{A}}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha)|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\geq\delta,\ |\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\leq 2\vartheta_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\alpha})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{2\sup_{\alpha\in\Theta_{A}}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha)|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta\right)

(7.20)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\alpha})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}).

By the uniform version on the Hájek-Renyi inequality in Lemma 2
of Iacus and Yoshida (2012),
we obtain

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{2\sup_{\alpha\in\Theta_{A}}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha)|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{2\sup_{\alpha\in\Theta_{A}}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha)|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta\vartheta_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{j>M/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}-1}\frac{2}{j}\sup_{\alpha}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+j}\left(\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)-\mathbb{E}[\partial_{\alpha}F_{i}(\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|\geq\delta\vartheta_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j>M/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}-1}\frac{C}{(\delta\vartheta_{\alpha}j)^{2}}

(7.21)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}}{(\delta\vartheta_{\alpha})^{2}}\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{M}=\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta^{2}M}=:\gamma_{\alpha}(M),

Noting that if

|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|\leq\vartheta_{\alpha}/2
holds,
then

|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\leq|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\leq 2\vartheta_{\alpha}
,
i.e.,

\displaystyle\{|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\alpha}\}\subset\left\{|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right\}\cup\left\{|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right\},

and for sufficiently large
n
so that

P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|>\vartheta_{\alpha}/2\right)<\epsilon/2

because of
\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*})=o_{p}(1)
,
we see

(7.22)

\displaystyle P(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\alpha})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)<\epsilon.

From
[C6-I]
and
[A2-I]
,
we have
\hat{\alpha}_{k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\alpha_{0}
as
n\to\infty
and

P(\hat{\alpha}_{k}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})<\epsilon
for large
n
.
Therefore, from (
7.20
)-(
7.22
) and this,
we have

P_{1,n}^{\alpha}\leq\gamma_{\alpha}(M)+3\epsilon
for large
n
.

[ii] Evaluation of
P_{2,n}^{\alpha}
.
If
\hat{\alpha}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-I_{d}\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-I_{d}\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left.\partial_{\alpha}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}}(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{3!}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{3}}{3!}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{3!}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{3}}{3!}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\alpha}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{0})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{3!}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{3}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{3}}{3!}\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{4}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\Bigr)\right|_{\alpha=\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 4}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|^{2},

where

\lambda_{1}[N]
denotes the minimum eigenvalue of a symmetric matrix
N
,
and
r_{i-1}
satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}r_{i-1}\right|=o_{p}(1)

from
[A1-I]
and
[A2-I]
.
Therefore we obtain

\displaystyle P_{2,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 2\delta,\ |\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\geq\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2},\ \hat{\alpha}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|^{2}\leq 2\delta,\ |\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\geq\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)\leq 8\delta\right)

(7.23)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right).

According to
[A2-I]
,
if we set

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{1}{19}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\lambda_{1}[\Xi^{\alpha}(x,\alpha_{0})]\mathrm{d}\mu_{\alpha_{0}}(x)>0,

then for large
n
,

\displaystyle P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)\leq 8\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\frac{1}{2}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]\leq 9\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}r_{i-1}\leq-\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]\leq 18\delta\right)+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}r_{i-1}\right|\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left(\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]-19\delta\right)\leq-\delta\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]-19\delta\right|\geq\delta\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{k>M/\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}-1}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+k}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]-19\delta\right|\geq\delta\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{[n^{1/r}]\leq k\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+k}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})]-19\delta\right|\geq\delta\right)+\epsilon

(7.24)

\displaystyle\leq 2\epsilon.

Noting that if

|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|\leq\vartheta_{\alpha}/4
holds,
then

\vartheta_{\alpha}=|\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\leq|\alpha_{1}^{*}-\hat{\alpha}_{1}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\leq|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|+\vartheta_{\alpha}/2
,
i.e.,

\displaystyle\left\{|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right\}\subset\left\{|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{4}\right\}\cup\left\{|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{4}\right\}

and for sufficiently large
n
so that

P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*}|>\vartheta_{\alpha}/4\right)<\epsilon/2

because of
\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*})=o_{p}(1)
,
we see

(7.25)

\displaystyle P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{4}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{4}\right)<\epsilon.

Therefore, from (
7.23
)-(
7.25
), we obtain

P_{2,n}^{\alpha}\leq 5\epsilon
for large
n
.

[iii] Evaluation of
P_{3,n}^{\alpha}
.
We have, for large
n
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-h^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\right)\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\right)\Bigr)+\left|A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right|\left|R_{i-1}(h,\theta)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1}}(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\mathrm{d}u\right||R_{i-1}(h,\theta)||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle=\left[\mathrm{tr}\left(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\right)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-v)\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+v(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\mathrm{d}u\right||R_{i-1}(h,\theta)||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle=\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-v)\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+v(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\mathrm{d}u\right||R_{i-1}(h,\theta)||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle=\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})+\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})\otimes(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-v)\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+v(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\mathrm{d}u\right||R_{i-1}(h,\theta)||\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\right|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{n}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\right|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\left(\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{n}\left|\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\right|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\left(\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\left|\int_{0}^{1}(1-v)\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{1}}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell_{3}}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}+v(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}))\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left(\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\right)^{2}\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+h\vartheta_{\alpha}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\alpha}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\mathrm{d}u\right|R_{i-1}(1,\theta)\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{\sqrt{n}}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\otimes\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2})\otimes\sqrt{n}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})+\left(\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{n}+h\vartheta_{\alpha}\right)R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Xi_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{0})\right|\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\hat{\alpha}_{2}|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\vartheta_{\alpha}}{n}+h\vartheta_{\alpha}\right)\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}\vartheta_{\alpha}}\right)+O_{p}(\sqrt{n}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2})+O_{p}(\vartheta_{\alpha})+O_{p}(T\vartheta_{\alpha})=o_{p}(1).

Hence, we see

\displaystyle P_{3,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq 3\epsilon

for large
n
.

[iv] From the evaluations in Steps [i]-[iii], we have

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)\leq\gamma_{\alpha}(M)+11\epsilon

for any
M\geq 1
and
\epsilon>0
.
Therefore

(7.26)

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)\leq 11\epsilon.

Since, for
\tau<\tau_{*}^{\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\Phi_{n}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})-\Phi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\Bigl(F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-F_{i}(\alpha_{1})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=[n\tau]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{1})-I_{d}\right)-\log\det A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{1})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=[n\tau]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{1})\bigl)\left(A_{i-1}(\alpha_{1})-h^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\Bigr),

we obtain, in the same way as above,

(7.27)

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\tau_{*}^{\alpha}-\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha})>M)\leq 11\epsilon.

We see, from (
7.18
), (
7.26
) and (
7.27
),

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}|\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha}|>M)\leq 22\epsilon
,
which shows

(7.28)

\displaystyle n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})=O_{p}(1).

From Lemmas
1
-
3
and (
7.28
),
we obtain

\displaystyle n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\longrightarrow}}\underset{v\in\mathbb{R}}{\mathrm{argmin}}\,\mathbb{F}(v).

This completes the proof of Theorem
1
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

As with the proof of Theorem
1
,
it is sufficient to show

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)=0.

Let
D_{n,M}^{\alpha}=\{\tau\in[0,1]|n(\tau-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\}
.
For all
\delta>0
,
we have

\displaystyle P\left(n(\hat{\tau}_{n}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle=:P_{1,n}^{\alpha}+P_{2,n}^{\alpha}+P_{3,n}^{\alpha}.

[i] Evaluation of
P_{1,n}^{\alpha}
.
For large
n
, we have

\displaystyle P_{1,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{\sup_{\alpha_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}}|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}).

By the uniform version on the Hájek-Renyi inequality in Lemma 2
of Iacus and Yoshida (2012),
we obtain

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sup_{\alpha_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}}|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})|\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{j>M-1}\frac{1}{j}\sup_{\alpha_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+j}\left(F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-\mathbb{E}[F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j>M-1}\frac{C}{(\delta j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta^{2}M}=:\gamma_{\alpha}(M).

From
[C6-I]
,
we have

P(\hat{\alpha}_{k}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}})<\epsilon
for large
n
.
Therefore
P_{1,n}^{\alpha}\leq\gamma_{\alpha}(M)+2\epsilon
for large
n
.

[ii] Evaluation of
P_{2,n}^{\alpha}
.
If
\hat{\alpha}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}
,
then there exists a positive constant
c
independent of
i
such that

\displaystyle\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})}\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\right|_{\alpha_{k}=\alpha_{k}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}\\
\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|).

According to
[B1-I]
,
if we set

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{1}{4}\inf_{x}\Gamma^{\alpha}(x,\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})>0,

then for large
n
,

\displaystyle P_{2,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\leq 2\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Bigl(\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|)\Bigr)\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|)\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})\leq 3\delta\right)+P\Bigl(-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|)\leq-\delta\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{x}\Gamma^{\alpha}(x,\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})\leq 3\delta\right)+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{c}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\epsilon

thanks to

\displaystyle P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{c}\right)\leq P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{2c}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{2c}\right)\leq 2\epsilon

from
[C6-I]
.

[iii] Evaluation of
P_{3,n}^{\alpha}
.

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-h^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})-hQ_{i-1}(\theta^{*})+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\right)\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl((A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha)\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+h|A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})||Q_{i-1}(\theta^{*})|+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\int_{0}^{1}\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl((A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha)\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{h}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}|A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})||Q_{i-1}(\theta^{*})|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{h^{2}}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sup_{x,\alpha_{k}}\left|\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl((A^{-1}(x,\alpha_{1})-A^{-1}(x,\alpha_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A(x,\alpha_{3})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+h\sup_{x,\alpha_{k}}\bigl|A^{-1}(x,\alpha_{1})-A^{-1}(x,\alpha_{2})\bigr|\sup_{x,\theta}|Q(x,\theta)|+\frac{h^{2}}{M}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{n}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

Hence, we see

\displaystyle P_{3,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq 3\epsilon

for large
n
.

[iv] From the evaluations in Steps [i]-[iii], we have

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)\leq\gamma_{\alpha}(M)+9\epsilon

for any
M\geq 1
and
\epsilon>0
.
Therefore

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)\leq 9\epsilon.

∎

Proof of Corollary
1
.

It is sufficient to show, for all
\epsilon_{1}\in[0,1)
and
M>0
,

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n^{\epsilon_{1}}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)=0.

Let
D_{n,M}^{\alpha}=\{\tau\in[0,1]|n^{\epsilon_{1}}(\tau-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\}
.
Suppose that there exists
\delta_{1}\in(0,\frac{1-\epsilon_{1}}{2})
such that

nh^{1/(\epsilon_{1}+\delta_{1})}\longrightarrow 0
.
We have

\displaystyle P\left(n^{\epsilon_{1}}(\hat{\tau}_{n}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq 1\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 2\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\varrho}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq 1\right)

\displaystyle=:P_{1,n}^{\alpha}+P_{2,n}^{\alpha}+P_{3,n}^{\alpha}.

[i] Evaluation of
P_{1,n}^{\alpha}
.
For large
n
, we have

\displaystyle P_{1,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq 1\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq 1,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{\sup_{\alpha_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}}|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\geq n^{-\delta_{1}}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}).

By the uniform version on the Hájek-Renyi inequality in Lemma 2
of Iacus and Yoshida (2012),
we obtain

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sup_{\alpha_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}}|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})|\geq n^{-\delta_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{j>M/n^{\epsilon_{1}-1}-1}\frac{1}{j}\sup_{\alpha_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+j}\left(F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})-\mathbb{E}[F_{i}(\alpha_{1})-F_{i}(\alpha_{2})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|\geq n^{-\delta_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j>M/n^{\epsilon_{1}-1}-1}\frac{C}{(n^{-\delta_{1}}j)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}}{n^{-2\delta_{1}}}\frac{n^{\epsilon_{1}-1}}{M}=\frac{C^{\prime}}{M}n^{\epsilon_{1}+2\delta_{1}-1}\longrightarrow 0.

From
[C6-I]
,
we have

P(\hat{\alpha}_{k}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}})<\epsilon
for large
n
.
Therefore
P_{1,n}^{\alpha}\leq 3\epsilon
for large
n
.

[ii] Evaluation of
P_{2,n}^{\alpha}
.
If
\hat{\alpha}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{k}^{*}}
,
then there exists a positive constant
c
independent of
i
such that

\displaystyle\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})

\displaystyle=\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})}\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})\right|_{\alpha_{k}=\alpha_{k}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{k}-\alpha_{k}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}\\
\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|).

We see, for large
n
,

\displaystyle P_{2,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\leq 2,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Bigl(\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|)\Bigr)\leq 2n^{-\delta_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|)\leq 2n^{-\delta_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Gamma_{i-1}^{\alpha}(\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})\leq 3n^{-\delta_{1}}\right)+P\Bigl(-c(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|)\leq-n^{-\delta_{1}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{x}\Gamma^{\alpha}(x,\alpha_{1}^{*},\alpha_{2}^{*})\leq 3{n^{-\delta_{1}}}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{n^{-\delta_{1}}}{c}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}\right)+P\left(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 5\epsilon

thanks to

\displaystyle P\left(|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{n^{-\delta_{1}}}{c}\right)\leq P\left(n^{\delta_{1}-1/2}\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{1}-\alpha_{1}^{*}|\geq\frac{1}{2c}\right)+P\left(n^{\delta_{1}-1/2}\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{1}{2c}\right)\leq 2\epsilon

from
[C6-I]
.

[iii] Evaluation of
P_{3,n}^{\alpha}
.

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-h^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})\right)\left(A_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2})-A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\right)\Bigr)+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl((A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha)\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h,\theta)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{-\delta_{1}+1/2}}\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\int_{0}^{1}\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl((A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{1})-A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha)\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h}{n^{-\delta_{1}}}\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{[n\tau]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{-\delta_{1}+1/2}}\sup_{x,\alpha_{k}}\left|\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl((A^{-1}(x,\alpha_{1})-A^{-1}(x,\alpha_{2}))\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A(x,\alpha_{3})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}_{2}-\alpha_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h}{n^{-\delta_{1}}}\frac{n^{\epsilon_{1}-1}}{M}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}]+1}^{n}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle=O_{p}(n^{\delta_{1}-1/2})+O_{p}(n^{\epsilon_{1}+\delta_{1}}h)=O_{p}(n^{\delta_{1}-1/2})+O_{p}(nh^{1/(\epsilon_{1}+\delta_{1})})=o_{p}(1).

Hence, we see

\displaystyle P_{3,n}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\alpha}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\alpha}(\tau:\hat{\alpha}_{1},\hat{\alpha}_{2})|}{n^{-\delta_{1}}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\alpha}])}\geq 1,\ \hat{\alpha}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\alpha}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq 3\epsilon

for large
n
.

[iv] From the evaluations in Steps [i]-[iii], we have

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(n^{\epsilon_{1}}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\alpha}-\tau_{*}^{\alpha})>M)\leq 11\epsilon

for any
M>0
and
\epsilon>0
.
∎

In Case A of Situation II, we set

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{n}(v)

\displaystyle=\Psi_{n}\left(\tau_{*}^{\beta}+\frac{v}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}:\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*}\middle|\alpha^{*}\right)-\Psi_{n}\left(\tau_{*}^{\beta}:\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*}\middle|\alpha^{*}\right),

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{n}(v)

\displaystyle=\Psi_{n}\left(\tau_{*}^{\beta}+\frac{v}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}\middle|\hat{\alpha}\right)-\Psi_{n}\left(\tau_{*}^{\beta}:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}\middle|\hat{\alpha}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{n}^{\beta}(v)

\displaystyle=\hat{\mathbb{G}}_{n}(v)-\mathbb{G}_{n}(v).

Suppose that
[C1]
-
[C5]
,
[C6-II]
,

[A1-II]
-
[A3-II]
hold.
Then, for all
L>0
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[-L,L]}|\mathcal{D}_{n}^{\beta}(v)|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0

as
n\to\infty
.

Proof.

We assume that
v>0
. Then, we can express

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{n}^{\beta}(v)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{1}|\hat{\alpha})-G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})\right)-\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\biggl(G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{1}|\alpha^{*})-G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}|\alpha^{*})+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\left(\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{1}|\alpha)-\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}|\alpha)\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Biggl(G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})+\sum_{j=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{j!}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*})^{\otimes j}-\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes j}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*})^{\otimes m}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes m}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})|\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}))\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\Biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad-\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\sum_{j=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{j!}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*})^{\otimes j}-\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes j}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\biggl(\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*})^{\otimes m}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes m}

(7.29)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})|\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}))\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\biggr).

Now we see

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{j}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{j}-\beta_{j}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})^{\otimes m}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\right||\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|^{m}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T^{m/2}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\sup_{\beta\in\Theta_{B}}\left|\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha^{*})\right|\left(\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|\right)^{m}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{T^{m/2}h^{1/2}\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\right)=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{n^{m/2-1}h^{(m-1)/2}}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\right)

(7.30)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})|\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}))\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})|\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}))\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\right||\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}||\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}\left|\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)\right|\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\sqrt{n}|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|

(7.31)

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\vartheta_{\beta}}\right)=o_{p}(1)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})^{\otimes j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})^{\otimes j}

(7.32)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})^{\otimes j}.

By Theorem 2.11 of Hall and Heyde (1980), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\frac{1}{T^{j}}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T^{j}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\left|\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right|^{2}\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}}{T}\frac{h}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}=\frac{C^{\prime}}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\longrightarrow 0

and

(7.33)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T^{j/2}}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|=o_{p}(1).

Moreover, we see

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right||\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(-2h\Bigl[\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{k}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{k}^{*}))\Bigr]_{\ell_{1}}+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\right)\right||\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\biggl(-2h\Bigl(\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{k}^{*})(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*})\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\Bigl[\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{k}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{k}^{*}+u(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*}))\Bigr]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\biggr)\Biggr||\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\biggl(-2h\Bigl(\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*})\right.

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{k}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*})\otimes(\beta_{k}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\Bigl[\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{k}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{k}^{*}+u(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*}))\Bigr]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\biggr)\Biggr||\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2h\vartheta_{\beta}}{\sqrt{T}}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\right|\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*}|\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*}|\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\beta_{k}^{*}-\beta_{0}|\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)(\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{k}^{*}|)^{2}\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\vartheta_{\beta}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\right)+O_{p}\left(\frac{h}{\sqrt{T}\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\right)

(7.34)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)

and, for
j\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{k}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right||\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|^{j}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(h,\theta)|\hat{\beta}_{j}-\beta_{j}^{*}|^{j}

\displaystyle=\frac{h}{T^{j/2}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)\left(\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|\right)^{j}

\displaystyle=O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\right)

(7.35)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

Therefore, from (
7.29
)-(
7.35
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}|{\mathcal{D}}_{n}^{\beta}(v)|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

By the similar proof, we see

\sup_{v\in[-L,0]}|{\mathcal{D}}_{n}^{\beta}(v)|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
,
and this proof is complete.
∎

Suppose that
[C1]
-
[C5]
,
[C6-II]
,

[A1-II]
-
[A3-II]
hold.
Then, for all
L>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{n}(v)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathbb{G}(v)\text{ in }\mathbb{D}[-L,L]

as
n\to\infty
.

Proof.

We consider
v>0
. We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{n}(v)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(G_{i}(\beta_{1}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Biggl(\sum_{j=1}^{m-1}\frac{1}{j!}\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes m}\Biggr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Biggl(\sum_{j=3}^{m-1}\frac{1}{j!}\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes j}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes m}\Biggr)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right)+\bar{o}_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=:\mathbb{G}_{1,n}(v)+\mathbb{G}_{2,n}(v)+\bar{o}_{p}(1),

where, for
j\geq 3
,

\displaystyle\vartheta_{\beta}^{j}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\vartheta_{\beta}^{j}\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}^{j}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right|=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{m-1}}{(m-1)!}\partial_{\beta}^{m}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})|\alpha^{*})\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes m}\right|=o_{p}(1).

Now, we see

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{1,n}(v)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})

(7.36)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}](\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*}),

(7.37)
