\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}](\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})\right|\leq\vartheta_{\beta}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)=O_{p}\left(\frac{h}{\vartheta_{\beta}}\right)=o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\Bigl(\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})\Bigr)^{2}\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]

\displaystyle=(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\biggl[\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)^{\mathsf{T}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)\biggr|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\biggr](\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle=(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(4h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\right)(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})

(7.38)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}4e_{\beta}^{\mathsf{T}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Xi^{\beta}(x,\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})}(x)e_{\beta}v=4\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}\left[\Bigl(\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})\Bigr)^{4}\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]

(7.39)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\vartheta_{\beta}^{4}R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

According to Corollary 3.8 of McLeish (1974),
we obtain, from (
7.38
) and (
7.39
),

(7.40)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}^{1/2}\mathcal{W}(v)\text{ in }\mathbb{D}[0,L].

Further, from (
7.36
), (
7.37
) and (
7.40
),
we have

\mathbb{G}_{1,n}(v)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}^{1/2}\mathcal{W}(v)
in
\mathbb{D}[0,L]
.

Besides, we see

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\Bigr)\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right|

(7.41)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|,

(7.42)

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\Bigr)\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0

and

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|

\displaystyle=\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(2h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)\right)\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|+o_{p}(1)

(7.43)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0,

where (
7.42
) is obtained by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\left(\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\right)\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\vartheta_{\beta}^{4}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+L/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\left|\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\left[\partial_{\beta}^{2}G_{i}(\beta_{2}^{*}|\alpha^{*})\middle|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}\right]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C^{\prime}\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}\longrightarrow 0

from Theorem 2.11 of Hall and Heyde (1980),
and (
7.43
) is obtained by

\displaystyle\sup_{v\in[0,L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{v\in[0,\epsilon_{n}]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{v\in[\epsilon_{n},L]}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq\epsilon_{n}\sup_{v\in[0,\epsilon_{n}]}\left|\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+L\sup_{v\in[\epsilon_{n},L]}\left|\frac{1}{v}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}+v/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}]}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}\right|

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}(\epsilon_{n})+L\max_{[n^{1/r}]\leq k\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+k}h\Xi^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*}))^{\otimes 2}-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}\right|

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1)

Here
\{\epsilon_{n}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
is a positive sequence such that

\epsilon_{n}\longrightarrow 0
and
\epsilon_{n}/\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}\longrightarrow\infty
,
and
r
is a constant with
r\in(1,2)
and
nh^{r}\longrightarrow\infty
.
From (
7.41
)-(
7.43
),
we have

\sup_{v\in[0,L]}|\mathbb{G}_{2,n}(v)-\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v|\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
.
Therefore we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{n}(v)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle w}}{{\longrightarrow}}-2\mathcal{J}_{\beta}^{1/2}\mathcal{W}(v)+\mathcal{J}_{\beta}v\ \text{ in }\ \mathbb{D}[0,L].

The argument for
v<0
is proved as well.
∎

Proof of Theorem
3
.

It is enough to show

(7.44)

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P\left(T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M\right)=0.

Because in the same way as the proof of Theorem
1
,
(
7.44
) leads to

T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})=O_{p}(1)
,
and this and Lemmas
1
,
4
,
5
yield

\displaystyle T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\longrightarrow}}\arg\min_{v\in\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}(v).

We show (
7.44
) below. For
\tau>\tau_{*}^{\beta}
, we have

\displaystyle\Psi_{n}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)-\Psi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\beta}:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau]}G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)+\sum_{i=[n\tau]+1}^{n}G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)-\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{n}G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Big(G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)\Bigr).

Now, from

\displaystyle G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)

\displaystyle=G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle\quad+h\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\left(b_{i-1}(\beta_{1})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2})\right)^{\otimes 2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+2\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\left(hb_{i-1}(\beta_{2})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\Delta X_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\left(b_{i-1}(\beta_{1})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2})\right)^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr),

we see

\displaystyle\Psi_{n}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)-\Psi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\beta}:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Bigl(G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+h\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\left(b_{i-1}(\beta_{1})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2})\right)^{\otimes 2}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\left(hb_{i-1}(\beta_{2})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\Delta X_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\left(b_{i-1}(\beta_{1})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2})\right)^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=:{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)+{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)+{\varrho}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha).

Let
M\geq 1
,

D_{n,M}^{\beta}=\{\tau\in[0,1]|T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\tau-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M\}
.
For all
\delta>0
, we have

\displaystyle P\left(T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\Psi_{n}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})\leq\Psi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\beta}:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left(\Psi_{n}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})-\Psi_{n}(\tau_{*}^{\beta}:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})\right)\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left({\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})+{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})+{\varrho}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})\right)\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})+{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})+{\varrho}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 0\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq-\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\varrho}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq-\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\varrho}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle=:P_{1,n}^{\beta}+P_{2,n}^{\beta}+P_{3,n}^{\beta}.

[i] Evaluation of
P_{1,n}^{\beta}
.
Choose
\epsilon>0
as you like.
Because
\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)

is continuous with respect to
\beta\in\Theta_{B}
,
we can choose
\bar{\beta}\in\mathcal{O}_{\hat{\beta}_{2}}
so that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\alpha)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}|\alpha)-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\hat{\beta}_{2}|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\bar{\beta}|\alpha)-\left.\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right|_{\beta=\bar{\beta}}\right)(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}).

If
\hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}}
and

\hat{\beta}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}
, then

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\bar{\beta}|\hat{\alpha})-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\bar{\beta}|\hat{\alpha})|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right||\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right||\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|

\displaystyle=:\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}|\mathbb{M}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta|\alpha)||\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|.

Hence we have

\displaystyle P_{1,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle=P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta,\ |\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\leq 2\vartheta_{\beta},\ \hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\beta})+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta|\alpha)|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\geq\delta,\ |\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\leq 2\vartheta_{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\beta})+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{2\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta|\alpha)|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)

(7.45)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\beta})+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}).

By the uniform version on the Hájek-Renyi inequality in Lemma 2
of Iacus and Yoshida (2012),
we obtain

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{2\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta|\alpha)|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle=P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{2\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}|{\mathbb{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta|\alpha)|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\geq\delta h\vartheta_{\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{j>M/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}-1}\frac{2}{j}\sup_{(\alpha,\beta)\in\Theta}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+j}\left(\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)-\mathbb{E}[\partial_{\beta}G_{i}(\beta|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|\geq\delta h\vartheta_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j>M/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}-1}\frac{Ch}{(\delta h\vartheta_{\beta}j)^{2}}

(7.46)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}h}{(\delta h\vartheta_{\beta})^{2}}\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{M}=\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta^{2}M}=:\gamma_{\beta}(M).

Noting that if

|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|\leq\vartheta_{\beta}/2
holds,
then

|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\leq|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|+|\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\leq 2\vartheta_{\beta}
,
i.e.,

\displaystyle\{|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\beta}\}\subset\left\{|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right\}\cup\left\{|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right\},

and for sufficiently large
n
so that

P(|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|>\vartheta_{\beta}/2)<\epsilon/2

because of
\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})=o_{p}(1)
,
we see

(7.47)

\displaystyle P(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|>2\vartheta_{\beta})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)<\epsilon.

From
[C6-II]
and
[A2-II]
,
we have

\hat{\alpha}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\alpha^{*}
and

\hat{\beta}_{k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\beta_{0}
as
n\to\infty
,
that is,

P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})<\epsilon
and

P(\hat{\beta}_{k}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})<\epsilon
for large
n
.
Therefore, from (
7.45
)-(
7.47
) and these, we have

P_{1,n}^{\beta}\leq\gamma_{\beta}(M)+4\epsilon
for large
n
.

[ii] Evaluation of
P_{2,n}^{\beta}
.
If
\hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}}
and
\hat{\beta}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}
,
then we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

\displaystyle=\left.\partial_{\beta}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}}(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{3!}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{3}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{3}}{3!}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{4}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 4}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\\
\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\end{subarray}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle=\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\hat{\beta}_{2})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{3!}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{3}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{3}}{3!}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{4}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 4}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\\
\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\end{subarray}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle=\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}+\partial_{\beta}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{3!}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{3}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 3}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\frac{(1-u)^{3}}{3!}\left.\partial_{\beta}^{4}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 4}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\otimes 2}\right)\right|_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\\
\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\end{subarray}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\geq\left(\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|^{2},

where
r_{i-1}
satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}r_{i-1}\right|=o_{p}(1)

from
[A1-II]
and
[A2-II]
. Therefore we have

\displaystyle P_{2,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle=P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 2\delta,\ |\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\geq\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2},\ \hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|^{2}\leq 2\delta,\ |\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\geq\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\beta_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)\leq 8\delta\right)

(7.48)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}).

According to
[A2-II]
,
if we set

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{1}{10}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\lambda_{1}[\Xi^{\beta}(x,\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]\mathrm{d}\mu_{(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})}(x)>0,

then for large
n
,

\displaystyle P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]+r_{i-1}\right)\leq 8\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]\leq 9\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}r_{i-1}\leq-\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]\leq 9\delta\right)+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}r_{i-1}\right|\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left(\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]-10\delta\right)\leq-\delta\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]-10\delta\right|\geq\delta\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{k>M/h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}-1}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+k}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]-10\delta\right|\geq\delta\right)+\epsilon

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{[n^{1/r}]\leq k\leq n}\left|\frac{1}{k}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+k}\lambda_{1}[\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})]-10\delta\right|\geq\delta\right)+\epsilon

(7.49)

\displaystyle\leq 2\epsilon.

Noting that if

|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|\leq\vartheta_{\beta}/4
holds,
then

\vartheta_{\beta}=|\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\leq|\beta_{1}^{*}-\hat{\beta}_{1}|+|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\leq|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|+\vartheta_{\beta}/2
,
i.e.,

\displaystyle\left\{|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right\}\subset\left\{|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{4}\right\}\cup\left\{|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{4}\right\}

and for sufficiently large
n
so that

P(|\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*}|>\vartheta_{\beta}/4)<\epsilon/2

because of
\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})=o_{p}(1)
,
we see

(7.50)

\displaystyle P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|<\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{4}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|>\frac{\vartheta_{\beta}}{4}\right)<\epsilon.

Therefore, from (
7.48
)-(
7.50
), we obtain

P_{2,n}^{\beta}\leq 6\epsilon
for large
n
.

[iii] Evaluation of
P_{3,n}^{\beta}
.
We have, for large
n
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})-h\mathbb{E}[\Delta X_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}])(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq h\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}))(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)|b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})|

\displaystyle=h\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1}}(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta)(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\right|_{\beta=\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)|b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})|

\displaystyle=h\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\beta)^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\right|_{\beta=\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta)(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\right|_{\beta=\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)|b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})|

\displaystyle=h\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\hat{\alpha},\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}))(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)|b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})|

\displaystyle=h\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{(\alpha,\beta)}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha,\beta)\right|_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\\
\beta=\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})\end{subarray}}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})\otimes\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}\\
\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad+h\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}))(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})^{\otimes 2}+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)|b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq h\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{\sqrt{T}}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{(\alpha,\beta)}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha,\beta)\right|_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\\
\beta=\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})\end{subarray}}\mathrm{d}u\right|\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|+|\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0}|)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{T}\left|\int_{0}^{1}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\left(\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\right)^{2}\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{\sqrt{T}}\left|\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+u(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\left(\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{T}\biggl|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\Bigl[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{2}}}\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{1}}}b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\partial_{\beta^{\ell_{3}}}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}+v(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\Bigr]_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2},\ell_{3}}\mathrm{d}u\mathrm{d}v\biggr|\left(\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\right)^{2}\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)|b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{\sqrt{T}}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\otimes\sqrt{T}(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})\otimes\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2})+\left(\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{\sqrt{nT}}+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{T}+h^{2}\right)R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\vartheta_{\beta}}\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\left|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Xi_{i-1}^{\beta}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})\right|\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\hat{\beta}_{2}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{\sqrt{nT}}+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{h\vartheta_{\beta}}{T}+h^{2}\right)\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle\leq O_{p}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}\vartheta_{\beta}}\right)+O_{p}(\sqrt{h}\vartheta_{\beta})+O_{p}(\sqrt{T}\vartheta_{\beta}^{2})+O_{p}(\vartheta_{\beta})+O_{p}(nh^{2})=o_{p}(1).

Hence, we see

\displaystyle P_{3,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{0}})

\displaystyle\leq 4\epsilon

for large
n
.

[iv] From the evaluations in Steps [i]-[iii], we have

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M)\leq\gamma_{\beta}(M)+14\epsilon

for any
M\geq 1
and
\epsilon>0
.
Therefore

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M)\leq 14\epsilon.

∎

Proof of Theorem
4
.

It is sufficient to show

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(T(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M)=0.

Let
M\geq 1
,

D_{n,M}^{\beta}=\{\tau\in[0,1]|T(\tau-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M\}
.
For all
\delta>0
, we have

\displaystyle P\left(T(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)+P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\quad+P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\varrho}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta\right)

\displaystyle=:P_{1,n}^{\beta}+P_{2,n}^{\beta}+P_{3,n}^{\beta}.

[i] Evaluation of
P_{1,n}^{\beta}
.
For large
n
, we have

\displaystyle P_{1,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})|}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}}.\ \hat{\beta}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{\sup_{\alpha\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\beta_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{k}^{*}}}|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)|}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\geq\delta h\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})+P(\hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})

By the uniform version on the Hájek-Renyi inequality in Lemma 2
of Iacus and Yoshida (2012),
we obtain

\displaystyle P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\\
\beta_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{k}^{*}}\end{subarray}}|{\mathcal{M}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\beta_{1},\beta_{2}|\alpha)|\geq\delta h\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\max_{j>M/h-1}\frac{1}{j}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\\
\beta_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{k}^{*}}\end{subarray}}\left|\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+j}\left(G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)-\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[G_{i}(\beta_{1}|\alpha)-G_{i}(\beta_{2}|\alpha)|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)\right|\geq\delta h\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j>M/h-1}\frac{Ch}{(\delta hj)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C^{\prime}h}{(\delta h)^{2}}\frac{h}{M}=\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta^{2}M}=:\gamma_{\beta}(M).

From
[C6-II]
,
we have

P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})<\epsilon
and

P(\hat{\beta}_{k}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{k}^{*}})<\epsilon
for large
n
.
Therefore

P_{1,n}^{\beta}\leq\gamma_{\beta}(M)+3\epsilon

for large
n
.

[ii] Evaluation of
P_{2,n}^{\beta}
.
If
\hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}}
and
\hat{\beta}_{k}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{k}^{*}}
,
then there exists a positive constant
c
independent of
i
such that

\displaystyle\Gamma^{\beta}_{i-1}(\hat{\alpha},\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2})

\displaystyle=\Gamma^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{(\alpha,\beta_{1},\beta_{2})}\Gamma^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha,\beta_{1},\beta_{2})\right|_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})\\
\beta_{k}=\beta_{k}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{k}-\beta_{k}^{*})\end{subarray}}\mathrm{d}u\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}\\
\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}\\
\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}\end{array}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\Gamma^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})-c(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|).

According to
[B1-II]
,
if we set

\displaystyle\delta=\frac{1}{4}\inf_{x}\Gamma^{\beta}(x,\alpha^{*},\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})>0,

then for large
n
,

\displaystyle P_{2,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2}|\hat{\alpha})}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\leq 2\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}\hat{\beta}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\left(\Gamma^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})-c\bigl(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\bigr)\right)\leq 2\delta\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}\Gamma^{\beta}_{i-1}(\alpha^{*},\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})\leq 3\delta\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P\left(-c\bigl(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\bigr)\leq-\delta\right)+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\inf_{x}\Gamma^{\beta}(x,\alpha^{*},\beta_{1}^{*},\beta_{2}^{*})\leq 3\delta\right)+P\left(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{c}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq 6\epsilon

thanks to

\displaystyle P\left(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|+|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{c}\right)

\displaystyle\leq P\left(|\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{3c}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{1}-\beta_{1}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{3c}\right)+P\left(|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|\geq\frac{\delta}{3c}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 3\epsilon

from
[C6-II]
.

[iii] Evaluation of
P_{3,n}^{\beta}
.
We have, for large
n
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})-h\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\Delta X_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}])(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=h\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2})-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}))(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)

\displaystyle=h\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\beta}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}))(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right|_{\beta=\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})+R_{i-1}(h^{2},\theta)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2})|}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\Biggl|\frac{1}{[n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\beta}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(b_{i-1}(\beta)-b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}))(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{1})-b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}_{2}))^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right|_{\beta=\beta_{2}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*})}\mathrm{d}u\Biggr|\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{h^{2}}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{[n\tau]}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sup_{x,\alpha,\beta_{k}}\left|\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}(x,\alpha)\partial_{\beta^{\ell}}b(x,\beta_{1})\bigl(b(x,\beta_{2})-b(x,\beta_{3})\bigr)^{\mathsf{T}}\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|\sqrt{T}|\hat{\beta}_{2}-\beta_{2}^{*}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{h^{2}}{M}\sum_{i=[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}]+1}^{n}R_{i-1}(1,\theta)

\displaystyle=o_{p}(1).

Hence, we see

\displaystyle P_{3,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle\leq P\left(\sup_{\tau\in D_{n,M}^{\beta}}\frac{|\varrho_{n}^{\beta}(\tau:\hat{\beta}_{1},\hat{\beta}_{2})|}{h([n\tau]-[n\tau_{*}^{\beta}])}\geq\delta,\ \hat{\alpha}\in\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{1}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}},\ \hat{\beta}_{2}\in\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+P(\hat{\alpha}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\alpha^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{1}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{1}^{*}})+P(\hat{\beta}_{2}\notin\mathcal{O}_{\beta_{2}^{*}})

\displaystyle\leq 4\epsilon

for large
n
.

[iv] From the evaluations in Steps [i]-[iii], we have

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(T(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M)\leq\gamma_{\beta}(M)+13\epsilon

for any
M\geq 1
and
\epsilon>0
.
Therefore

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{M\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}P(T(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{\beta}-\tau_{*}^{\beta})>M)\leq 13\epsilon.

∎

Proof of Proposition
1
.

We see

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{n}^{\alpha}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2dn}}\max_{1\leq k\leq n}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\hat{\eta}_{i}-\frac{k}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\eta}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{2dn}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\hat{\eta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\eta}_{i}\right|

(7.51)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}{2d}}\left|\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\hat{\eta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\eta}_{i}\right|.

Now we can express

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{i}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)+\left.\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}}(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.(1-u)\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)+\left(\mathrm{tr}\left(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}AA^{-1}_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)\right)_{\ell}(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle=:\eta_{1,i}+\eta_{2,i}(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\mathrm{tr}\left(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)\frac{(\Delta X_{i})^{\otimes 2}}{h}\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})}\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})^{\otimes 2}.

Therefore we have, from
[E1]
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\hat{\eta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\eta}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\eta_{1,i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\eta_{1,i}+\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\eta_{2,i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\eta_{2,i}\right)\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})+o_{p}(1)

(7.52)

\displaystyle=:\mathcal{H}_{1,n}+\mathcal{H}_{2,n}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha_{0})+o_{p}(1).

Here
\mathcal{H}_{1,n}
and
\mathcal{H}_{2,n}
can be transformed as follows.

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1,n}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\eta_{1,i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\eta_{1,i},

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2,n}

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\eta_{2,i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\eta_{2,i}.

We have

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[\eta_{1,i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\eta_{1,i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathrm{tr}(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{1}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathrm{tr}(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}))+O_{p}(h/\vartheta_{\alpha})

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\biggl(d+\left.\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha))\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}}(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha))\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{0})}\otimes(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{0})^{\otimes 2}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\biggl(d+\left.\partial_{\alpha}\mathrm{tr}(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha))\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}}(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\left.\partial_{\alpha}^{2}\mathrm{tr}(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha))\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})}\otimes(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})^{\otimes 2}\biggr)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{0})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\bigl(A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell}\vartheta_{\alpha}^{-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\alpha}_{*}(1-\tau^{\alpha}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A(x,\alpha_{0})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell}\mathrm{d}\mu_{\alpha_{0}}(x)(c_{1}-c_{2})

and

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{(n\vartheta_{\alpha})^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[\eta_{1,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{(n\vartheta_{\alpha})^{2}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\eta_{1,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}}\left(\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[\eta_{1,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\eta_{1,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Therefore, from Lemma 9 of Genon-Catalot and Jacod (1993), we obtain

(7.53)

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1,n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\alpha}_{*}(1-\tau^{\alpha}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Bigl(\mathrm{tr}\bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A(x,\alpha_{0})\bigr)\Bigr)_{\ell}\mathrm{d}\mu_{\alpha_{0}}(x)(c_{1}-c_{2}).

Further, we have

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[\eta_{2,i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\eta_{2,i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}AA_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{1}^{*})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}AA_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha_{2}^{*})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}+O_{p}(h)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\biggl(\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}AA_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha)\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{0})}(\alpha_{1}^{*}-\alpha_{0})\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\biggl(\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}AA_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha_{0})A_{i-1}(\alpha)\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\right|_{\alpha=\alpha_{0}+u(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})}(\alpha_{2}^{*}-\alpha_{0})\biggr)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A_{i-1}(\alpha_{0})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0

and

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[\eta_{2,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\eta_{2,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left(\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{1}^{*}}[\eta_{2,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\alpha_{2}^{*}}[\eta_{2,i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Therefore, from Lemma 9 of Genon-Catalot and Jacod (1993), we see

\mathcal{H}_{2,n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0
.
Hence, from (
7.52
), (
7.53
) and this, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}\hat{\eta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\alpha}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\eta}_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\alpha}_{*}(1-\tau^{\alpha}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left[\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(A^{-1}\partial_{\alpha^{\ell}}A(x,\alpha_{0})\Bigr)\right]_{\ell}\mathrm{d}\mu_{\alpha_{0}}(x)(c_{1}-c_{2})\neq 0,

which implies
\mathcal{T}_{n}^{\alpha}\longrightarrow\infty

from (
7.51
) and
n\vartheta_{\alpha}^{2}\longrightarrow\infty
,
that is,

P(\mathcal{T}_{n}^{\alpha}>w_{1}(\epsilon))\longrightarrow 1
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
2
.

We see

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{1,n}^{\beta}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{dT}}\max_{1\leq k\leq n}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{k}\hat{\xi}_{i}-\frac{k}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\xi}_{i}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{dT}}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\xi}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\xi}_{i}\right|

(7.54)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}{d}}\left|\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\xi}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\xi}_{i}\right|.

Now we can express

\displaystyle\hat{\xi}_{i}

\displaystyle=1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))

\displaystyle=1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

\displaystyle=1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))-h1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

\displaystyle=:\xi_{i}-h\int_{0}^{1}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*}))\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\xi}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\xi}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\xi_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{i}+o_{p}(1)

(7.55)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\xi_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\xi_{i}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}[\xi_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\xi_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}h1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{1}^{*})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}h1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))+O_{p}(h/\vartheta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=-\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\biggl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}\biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\biggl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}\biggr)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=-\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\beta}_{*}(1-\tau^{\beta}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a^{-1}(x,\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{(\alpha^{*},\beta_{0})}(x)(d_{1}-d_{2}),

and

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{(T\vartheta_{\beta})^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}[\xi_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{(T\vartheta_{\beta})^{2}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\xi_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\left(\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}[\xi_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\xi_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Therefore, from Lemma 9 of Genon-Catalot and Jacod (1993), we see

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\xi_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\beta}_{*}(1-\tau^{\beta}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a^{-1}(x,\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\beta_{0}}(x)(d_{1}-d_{2}).

Hence, from (
7.55
) and this, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\xi}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\xi}_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\beta}_{*}(1-\tau^{\beta}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}1_{d}^{\mathsf{T}}a^{-1}(x,\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\beta_{0}}(x)(d_{1}-d_{2})\neq 0,

which implies
\mathcal{T}_{1,n}^{\beta}\longrightarrow\infty

from (
7.54
) and
T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}\longrightarrow\infty
,
that is,

P(\mathcal{T}_{1,n}^{\beta}>w_{1}(\epsilon))\longrightarrow 1
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
3
.

We see

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}_{2,n}^{\beta}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\max_{1\leq k\leq n}\left\|\mathcal{I}_{n}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{k}\hat{\zeta}_{i}-\frac{k}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\zeta}_{i}\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\mathcal{I}_{n}^{-1/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\zeta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\zeta}_{i}\right)\right\|

(7.56)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\left\|\mathcal{I}_{n}^{-1/2}\left(\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\zeta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\zeta}_{i}\right)\right\|.

Now we can express

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{i}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\hat{\alpha})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))

\displaystyle=\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

\displaystyle=\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))-h\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

\displaystyle=\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{M-1}\partial_{\beta}^{j}\left.\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta)^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))\Bigr)\right|_{\beta=\bar{\beta}^{*}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}^{M}\left.\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta)^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))\Bigr)\right|_{\beta=\bar{\beta}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})}\otimes(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes M}

\displaystyle\qquad-h\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*}))\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})

\displaystyle=:\zeta_{i}+\sum_{j=1}^{M-1}\partial_{\beta}^{j}\left.\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta)^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))\Bigr)\right|_{\beta=\bar{\beta}^{*}}\otimes(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}^{M}\left.\Bigl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta)^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))\Bigr)\right|_{\beta=\bar{\beta}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})}\otimes(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\otimes M}

\displaystyle\qquad-h\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}+u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*}))\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\beta}-\bar{\beta}^{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}\left.\partial_{\alpha}\left(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\hat{\beta})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha)(\Delta X_{i}-hb_{i-1}(\hat{\beta}))\right)\right|_{\alpha=\alpha^{*}+u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*})}\mathrm{d}u(\hat{\alpha}-\alpha^{*}).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\zeta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\zeta}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\zeta_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\zeta_{i}+o_{p}(1)

(7.57)

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\zeta_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\zeta_{i}+o_{p}(1),

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}[\zeta_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\zeta_{i}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}h\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{1}^{*})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}h\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{2}^{*})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))+O_{p}(h/\vartheta_{\beta})

\displaystyle=\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})(b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})-b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=-\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\biggl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\biggl(\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int_{0}^{1}(1-u)\partial_{\beta}^{2}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0}+u(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0}))\mathrm{d}u\otimes(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})^{\otimes 2}\biggr)

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=-\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{1}^{*}-\beta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]+1}^{n}\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\bar{\beta}^{*})^{\mathsf{T}}A_{i-1}^{-1}(\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b_{i-1}(\beta_{0})\vartheta_{\beta}^{-1}(\beta_{2}^{*}-\beta_{0})+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\beta}_{*}(1-\tau^{\beta}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})^{\mathsf{T}}A^{-1}(x,\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\beta_{0}}(x)(d_{1}-d_{2}),

and

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{(T\vartheta_{\beta})^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}[\zeta_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{(T\vartheta_{\beta})^{2}}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\zeta_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}}\left(\left(1-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{1}^{*}}[\zeta_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]-\left(\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\right)^{2}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{i=[n\tau^{*}]+1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\beta_{2}^{*}}[\zeta_{i}^{2}|\mathscr{G}_{i-1}^{n}]\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}0.

Therefore, from Lemma 9 of Genon-Catalot and Jacod (1993), we see

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\zeta_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\zeta_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\beta}_{*}(1-\tau^{\beta}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})^{\mathsf{T}}A^{-1}(x,\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\beta_{0}}(x)(d_{1}-d_{2})

Hence, from (
7.57
) and this, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}\hat{\zeta}_{i}-\frac{[n\tau^{\beta}_{*}]}{n}\frac{1}{T\vartheta_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hat{\zeta}_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\tau^{\beta}_{*}(1-\tau^{\beta}_{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})^{\mathsf{T}}A^{-1}(x,\alpha^{*})\partial_{\beta}b(x,\beta_{0})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\beta_{0}}(x)(d_{1}-d_{2})\neq 0,

which implies
\mathcal{T}_{2,n}^{\beta}\longrightarrow\infty

from (
7.56
) and
T\vartheta_{\beta}^{2}\longrightarrow\infty
,
that is,

P(\mathcal{T}_{2,n}^{\beta}>w_{q}(\epsilon))\longrightarrow 1
.
∎
